Outputs for cycle 1 are the state at start of cycle 1? Wait: outputs are recorded in step 3, which is after inputs take trace values and gates settle, but *before* clock edge? Let's read carefully:

"for each cycle t = 0 .. cycles-1, in order:
1. input nets take their trace values for cycle t. dff q nets hold their current state (at t=0, their init values).
2. all gates settle instantly.
3. the value of every net in outputs is recorded. this is the observed trace for cycle t.
4. clock edge: every dff simultaneously loads the settled value of its d net."

So at cycle 0, step 1: inputs take trace[0] values, DFFs hold init. Step 2: gates settle, computing d values. Step 3: outputs recorded. The outputs are q12, q15, q17, q4, q5, q6. These are DFF q nets. At step 3, they still hold their state from step 1 (init for t=0, or previous state for t>0). The new d values are loaded at step 4. So the recorded output for cycle t is the state *before* the clock edge.

Thus for cycle 0, outputs are init: q12=1, q15=1, q17=1, q4=1, q5=1, q6=1.
For cycle 1, outputs are the state at start of cycle 1, which is the d values from cycle 0, i.e., the state we just computed as "state at start of cycle 1": q12=1, q15=1, q17=0, q4=0, q5=1, q6=1.
Wait, my computed next state from cycle 0: q12=1, q15=1, q17=0, q4=0, q5=1, q6=1. Yes.
So output cycle 1: q12=1, q15=1, q17=0, q4=0, q5=1, q6=1.
